{"forms":[{"form":"disguises","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"A cape and moustache completed his disguise.","type":"example"},{"text":"hiding one's treacherous intentions under the guise of friendship","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Material (such as clothing, makeup, a wig) used to alter one’s visual appearance in order to hide one's identity or assume another."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"old ideas in a new guise","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The appearance of something on the outside which masks what’s beneath."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Any disguise may expose soldiers to be deemed enemy spies.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act or state of disguising, notably as a ploy."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A change of behaviour resulting from intoxication, drunkenness."],"tags":["archaic","countable","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/dɪsˈɡaɪz/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɪzˈɡaɪz/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/dɪsˈɡaɪz/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/dɪˈskaɪz/","tags":["General-American"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"camouflage"},{"word":"guise"},{"word":"mask"},{"word":"pretense"},{"word":"garb"}],"upstream_line":29414,"upstream_offset":196013011,"upstream_record_sha256":"e028c3988e6c76dffe8285265f7c20dcc469044b299d608020e356c9d514c77f","word":"disguise"}
